- **Fire values are the SDK's (verified, not changed):** SMG1 GetFireRate 0.075 s, bursts 2-5, range 1400; AR2 0.1 s, 2-5, 65-2048 (weapon_smg1.cpp, weapon_ar2.cpp); session 24's per-process native recording already matched the 0.075 s sound runs. One SDK difference found: CWeaponSMG1::Equip gives CLASS_PLAYER_ALLY owners 3000 units of range (fixed). Retail server.dll constants were not read (SDK = ours; the native recordings agree).
- **Distance variants (the "distant gunfire" and likely the "saw" report):** the installed scripts mark NPC SMG1/AR2/pistol shots, functank, mortar, explosions (BaseExplosionEffect.Sound, water explosions), turrets, sniper, strider footsteps, gunship/helicopter rotors and Breen's plaza lines with `^` (CHAR_DISTVARIANT: stereo wave, left = near, right = far). Retail engine.dll (no snd_dvar convars in this build): SND_Spatialize 1008f4d0 sets distmix = (clamp(dist, 240, 1320) - 240) / 1080 (constants 1031da0c/1031da20/1031d8f0), and the '^' mixers 100a3070/100a3840 mix near + (far - near) x distmix into ONE mono source. Ours played the stereo file as stereo (near in the left ear, far in the right) at every distance. Now the host preloads 17 mono premixes per stereo `^` wave and plays the one for the listener distance at the start (packaged trace: `npc_smg1_fire1.wav#dv0..#dv4` at 0-500 units). Limits: the mix is chosen once per sound (loops such as rotors do not update), no stereo panning and no DSP rooms yet (separate retail code paths, not researched). Native A/B: cadence matches the native plaza recording (in-run 0.06-0.09 s gaps: ours 62% of 200 shots, native 56% of 127 onsets); a band-spectrum fit of the near/far mix was **inconclusive** (it spreads our known 0.13-0.31 mixes over 0-0.96; private record_ours.py/fit_mix.py); next: native recordings at controlled near (< 240) and far (> 1320) distances.
- **Move and shoot (CAI_MoveAndShootOverlay, ai::moveshoot):** continuous-move tasks of bits_CAP_MOVE_SHOOT NPCs (soldier, companions, cops) switch to the weapon's ACT_WALK_AIM/ACT_RUN_AIM sequences (preloaded per weapon with the locomotion variants), the body faces the enemy LKP (AddFacingTarget 0.8 s; the legs keep the path via move_yaw) and the shot regulator fires (soldiers get a move-only regulator since CNPC_Combine has no bits_CAP_USE_SHOT_REGULATOR; CNPC_Combine::ShouldMoveAndShoot's danger-sound schedules and the hide-and-reload stop after U(0.4, 0.6) s for 5 s). Native restarts the ACT_GESTURE_RANGE_ATTACK1 gesture per shot; AI gesture layers do not exist yet, so the weapon's fire event is dispatched directly (no upper-body recoil gesture). Packaged (artifacts/s25/ms-smg, ms-cap.png): a foe soldier hit by a friend rebel runs to the alcove sideways with his gun on the rebel and fires 5 shots on the way; AR2 elite: shots while running to reload cover.
- **Alyx gun:** no fire spec existed. ## Current session 24, first part (2026-10-20)

Claude Code desktop, `claude-opus-5-5`. No subagents. Branch `wip/npc-ai-20261014`, not on main. Owner reports first (AR2 tracer, shotgun reload, NPC fire rate/sound), then the F4 grenade option the owner asked for.
- **AR2 tracer drew a black box (owner report):** `effects/gunshiptracer` is a `Sprite` material with `$spriterendermode 8` (kRenderTransAlphaAdd) and no `$additive`, so our sprite loader treated it as opaque and drew its DXT5 texture's black background. sprite_dx9.cpp draws mode 8 in two passes (src*a + dst*(1-a), then src*(1-a) + dst) that sum to src + dst*(1-a); new effect kind 5 blends One / OneMinusSrcAlpha without the alpha discard. Only gunshiptracer (and bluelaser1, already overridden) use a sprite render mode among our effect materials. Owned test; packaged AR2 soldier burst (artifacts/s24/ar2-*.png, crop ar2-tracer-crop.png): a glowing streak, no quad. Native AR2 look still not compared.
- **Combine soldiers reloaded forever (owner report, shotgun):** combine_soldier_anims `reload`/`reload_low` carry event 2 = COMBINE_AE_RELOAD, which CNPC_Combine::HandleAnimEvent handles (RELOAD_NPC, both clips full, ammo conditions cleared); we ignored it, so every soldier's clip stayed empty and SCHED_RELOAD repeated. Citizens (3015) and cops (3017/3018) use weapon events we already handled. Also ported: RunAttackTask's GetShotRegulator()->Reset(false) at the end of TASK_RELOAD. Packaged (artifacts/s24/sgp): a shotgun soldier fires to 1 shell, reloads to 6 (Weapon_Shotgun.NPC_Reload), three full cycles.
- **NPC SMG1/AR2 fire too slow and too regular (owner report).** Native evidence: a native SMG1 soldier recording (native per-process recording) has Weapon_SMG1.NPC_Single onsets in runs 0.075 s apart (13.3 Hz) with 0.15-0.47 s gaps; ours fired one sound every 0.21-0.315 s. Four SDK causes, all ported: (1) `CBaseAnimating::StudioFrameAdvanceInternal` marks a non-looping sequence finished once its cycle passes `GetLastVisibleCycle` (fadeouttime 0.2 s before the end), so the 0.133 s soldier and 0.28 s citizen shoot sequences finish after one 0.1 s think and NPCs fire every think; `DispatchAnimEvents` then fires the sequence's remaining events (flEnd 1.01). (2) `CHLMachineGun::WeaponSoundRealtime` (SMG1, AR2) emits up to two SINGLE_NPC sounds per shot, GetFireRate apart, to fill the next think (delayed starts are queued in the AI runtime). (3) SINGLE_NPC is CHAN_WEAPON: a new shot sound replaces the previous one (native run level 0.94x a single attack, 1.19x if tails stacked). (4) `CNPC_Combine` never has bits_CAP_USE_SHOT_REGULATOR: its own TASK_RANGE_ATTACK1 fires GetRandomBurst() shots (2-5) and the schedule's TASK_WAIT_RANDOM 0.3 makes the gap; our soldiers used the companion/cop regulator. Also `CAI_BaseHumanoid::BuildScheduleTestBits` (no COND_CAN_RANGE_ATTACK1 interrupt while the regulator rests). Packaged soldier in the station hall (artifacts/s24/smg2): 155 sound plays in 19.7 s (before: 99), runs 0.075 s apart, burst gaps 0.13-0.27 s (native ~0.23-0.47 s, median 0.35), reload pauses ~2.1 s (native ~4 s: reload schedule differences not investigated). Native AR2 recording rejected (a map cop walked into the soldier's line of fire). Citizens/cops: same finish rule (they now also shoot every think within regulator bursts); not compared with native.
- **Face/attack tasks aimed at the true enemy position:** they now use the LKP (enemy memory, which follows the enemy while seen and AI_FREE_KNOWLEDGE_DURATION 1.75 s after) like GetEnemyLKP; TASK_STORE_ENEMY_POSITION_IN_SAVEPOSITION keeps GetAbsOrigin.
- **F4 grenades (owner request):** the options column of npc_combine_s (the only HL2 class that reads NumGrenades) has `grenades: N (NumGrenades)` after the allegiance; Enter cycles 0, 1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 99; the spawn sets the soldier's m_iNumGrenades. Native npc_create soldiers have 0. Finding the first packaged throw needed three fixes: (a) every flush toss failed because the trace from the target (the enemy's last seen feet, on the floor) reported a hit at its own start (Source ignores a start-solid surface: only fraction matters); (b) combine_soldier has no ACT_RANGE_ATTACK2 sequence: CNPC_Combine::NPC_TranslateActivity maps it to ACT_COMBINE_THROW_GRENADE (grenThrow, event 7 at 0.6), now translated and preloaded; (c) Weapon_SetActivity again gets the full SequenceDuration. New diagnostics `ai.diagnostics.grenade_checks` (out_of_range, moving, no_throw, no_toss, ready_throw, ready_toss). Packaged (artifacts/s24/gh, fixture f4-grenade-hall.json; capture burst ghc-tile.png): an F4 SMG1 soldier with 5 grenades in the station hall corridor shoots the walking player, the player steps sideways out of sight (spot found with a temporary owned BSP probe: soldier (-3356,-2300), seen (-3356,-2100), hidden (-3516,-2100)), he selects SCHED_COMBINE_RANGE_ATTACK2, throws (5 -> 4), the frag blips, bounces and explodes 3.57 s later near the last seen spot. Native flush timing not compared.
- Fly (noclip) players are still not sensed by our AI (test aid; native noclip players are); enemy-facing tests must walk.
- Tests: hl2-simulation 317, source-assets 94, modkit-core 59; workspace release tests (only the two known release-only should_panic image tests fail); owned tests (HL2_ROOT, --ignored) 53 pass; strict Clippy and fmt clean. Code commit 155d455.
- Regression batch artifacts/s24-regression: weapons 17/17, attention 26/26, movement 25/26 (the known doll01 PHY warning only). Every final and attention capture matches s23-regression within 5 levels except 5-6 lip-sync pixels on Kleiner's scene/feed. Next batches diff against s24-regression.
- Likely reason for native's ~4 s reload pauses (not verified): SCHED_HIDE_AND_RELOAD -> SCHED_COMBINE_HIDE_AND_RELOAD runs to cover first on the plaza; our hall soldier fell back to SCHED_RELOAD in place.

## Current session 23 (2026-10-20)

Claude Code desktop, `claude-opus-5-5`. No subagents. Branch `wip/npc-ai-20261014` (pushed: 6d24cf9, 2ae8a88, b6343c5, f6c0525, 8df9a54 docs, 54b32b1 grenades). Not on main. Concurrent Codex documentation edits (scene-start Frida observation, campaign scene research, the d2_coast_02 note) remain separate and uncommitted for their owner.
- **Owner report: Breen moved his eyes randomly during the broadcast (since the session-17 random look queue).** Cause: both maps play the broadcast through `ss_breencast` (scripted_sequence, spawnflags 480 = no interrupt, override state, no teleport, SF_SCRIPT_LOOP_IN_POST_IDLE, post idle `sitpose`); the breencast VCDs' LookAt camera_tv_breen channel is disabled, so nothing else holds his eyes. SDK: during SCRIPT_POST_IDLE the NPC keeps m_hCine and NPC_STATE_SCRIPT until PostIdleDone (never while it loops), and MaintainLookTargets picks random targets only outside NPC_STATE_SCRIPT. Ours cleared the script owner when the action ended. Now the actor keeps a post-idle script state (one cycle, or until cancelled / another script takes it with the loop flag); only the random look gate reads it (choreography MOVETO/sequence ownership unchanged). Regression batch: Breen's feed eyes now hold forward (artifacts/s23/diff-partition-breen-screen-feed-final.png, s22 left vs s23 right). Native broadcast frames were not recaptured.
- **Muzzle-flash elights (owner detail item; parked after the owner's 2026-10-20 note: NPC work first).** Retail engine.dll research: CL_AllocElight slots, linear radius decay over 0.05 s, dlight -> dworldlight_t (intensity rgb * 2^exp / 255, quadratic 1 / (radius^2 * max(minlight, floor))), ComputeDynamicLighting slotting (dynamic lights always evict the weakest local light into the ambient cube). The minlight floor is set per map by CMapLoadHelper::Init from LUMP_MAP_FLAGS (HL2 maps with 0x200: 0.01953125); an earlier draft read it as a 1.0 constant, which made the viewmodel glow white (rejected capture artifacts/s23/elight-fire.png). Implemented for the player viewmodel (DoMuzzleFlash: pistol, SMG1, AR2, 357, shotgun, AR2 ball) and NPC weapons (DoMuzzleFlash except the AR2; MuzzleFlash_Combine_NPC blue). A native 60 fps pistol recording at the hall spot shows no visible glow on the viewmodel; ours with the per-map floor is faint (artifacts/s23/elight-fire2.png). Frame-by-frame decay and NPC elights: unit-tested only.
- **Stunstick melee against NPCs:** a swing that misses the player tests hated NPC hulls (CTraceFilterMelee D_HT) and applies sk_npc_dmg_stunstick (DMG_CLUB) through the shared hit path, with the 200-unit combat sound. Found and fixed: (a) against NPC enemies cops looped SCHED_METROPOLICE_ACTIVATE_BATON -> TASK_FACE_TARGET fail -> SCHED_FAIL because our target was always the player (SDK: SetTarget(GetEnemy())); (b) **sk_npc_dmg_stunstick was stored in a thread-local on the map-load worker thread, so every packaged baton hit (the player's included) dealt 0**; the runtime now carries it. Packaged F4 baton duel (artifacts/s23/baton-duel3): the friend cop activates its baton, closes in and kills the foe cop (40 damage vs 40 health; skill.cfg: "Kill a citizen in one hit").
- **Combine kick:** MeleeAttack1Conditions (64 units, facing 0.7, height 64, no headcrabs, center line) and COMBINE_AE_KICK (70-unit hull trace, sk_combine_s_kick 10, ViewPunch(-12,-7,0), forward*100 + up*50, NPC_Combine.WeaponBash, COMBINE_KICK sentence; COMBINE_* sentences preloaded; retail ships no kick1/kick2 waves, so the sentence is silent like retail). Packaged (artifacts/s23/kick4): a foe soldier kicks the player twice; the knock-back puts the player out of range and he switches to SCHED_COMBINE_RANGE_ATTACK1.
- **NPCs react to being hit:** every bullet/club hit on an NPC reaches its AI (OnTakeDamage_Alive): COND_LIGHT_DAMAGE, COND_HEAVY_DAMAGE above 20, and a hated player attacker becomes the enemy. Before, only NPC attackers reached the AI. Packaged (artifacts/s23/hurt): a soldier shot in the back turns Combat at once, tries cover and returns fire.
- **Combine flush grenades (54b32b1):** squadless CNPC_Combine: NumGrenades keyvalue (0 by default, so npc_create/F4 soldiers throw none, like native), CanGrenadeEnemy(false) -> CanThrowGrenade (128..1024 units, not moving, m_flNextGrenadeCheck) -> CheckCanThrowGrenade (VecCheckThrow at 650 in view and visible, else VecCheckToss with a 300-unit apex and 64 units of headroom); the flush rule (enemy out of sight at most 3 s, within 256 units of where it was seen) picks SCHED_COMBINE_RANGE_ATTACK2 (face the toss, ACT_RANGE_ATTACK2, wait in cover); COMBINE_AE_GREN_TOSS spawns a frag from "lefthand" (sk_npc_dmg_fraggrenade 75, radius 250, 3.5 s fuse), next check +6 s. Unit-tested (toss math lands on target, flush selection, toss request); owned tests pass after the ACT_RANGE_ATTACK2 preload; **a packaged throw by a map soldier is not tested yet** (needs a map with NumGrenades soldiers, e.g. the canals/coast). Not modeled: squads/slots, the throw's 4-unit hull check, grenade-before-reload (20%), turret cases.

## Cloud session handoff (2026-10-10, `wip/cloud-physics-20261010`)

Claude Code cloud session, model `claude-opus-5-5` per the session environment. No subagents. **New branch at the owner's request** ("physics in general": masses, materials, bounciness, impact sounds, the frag as a real physics object, +USE pickup of small props), cut from `wip/cloud-arsenal-20261010` fd9b8dc because the frag and the gravity gun live there; the arsenal branch's later commits (13b 3-5) are merged in (a5965df), so **merge order: arsenal first, then this branch**. The harness-assigned branch `claude/laughing-meitner-lkf1cm` was not used (the owner's prompt names the wip/* branches). SDK 2013 b8cfb12; VPhysics itself (vphysics.dll) is not in the SDK, so solver-level behavior is Rapier with SDK-side rules around it. **Unit tests, strict Clippy and fmt on Linux; hl2-bevy compiles and its tests pass on Linux, never run.**

Commits: 15c04e3 authored solids and materials; 0289ba6 impact sounds, air drag, frag rigid body; 6bd4750 +USE carry and shared grab controller; a5965df merge of the arsenal branch (+ carry/viewmodel-effects and carry/sprint glue).
- **Authored solids (13b/13c 1):** `source_assets::phy::{read_keyvalues, solids, first_solid}` read any solid count's KeyValues; the first "solid" block (PhysModelParseSolid with index -1) gives mass, inertia, damping, rotdamping, surfaceprop, volume with g_PhysDefaultObjectParams defaults (1, 1, 0.1, 0.1, "default"); a repeated key keeps its last value (assumption: vphysics.dll's parser assigns in order). `source_assets::surfaceprops` resolves scripts/surfaceproperties*.txt with "base" inheritance (no base: "default") into `World::surface_materials`; `models::append_models` reads each prop_physics model's solid into `World::model_physics` and, when the identity-root single-solid reader supports it, its convex pieces. Rapier: dynamic prop bodies use the authored pieces (else the old render hull), the authored mass on the body (uniform density over the pieces, the whole tensor x the "inertia" multiplier), massScale/inertiaScale keyvalues (CPhysicsProp::CreateVPhysics; inertia at least 0.5), linear/angular damping from the solid, and the surfaceprop's friction/elasticity combined by product (frag fit: grenade 0.9 x concrete 0.8); world brushes/terrain use the "default" surfaceprop (brush sides carry no material here). The gravity gun's punt/deny/launch now see real masses (body.mass()).
- **Impact sounds, drag (13b/13c 2):** `Physics::tick` applies dv/dt = -c|v|v with c = 6.4e-4/unit (the frag fit, **native fit needed** per object; `Physics::air_drag`) to awake dynamic bodies and reports new contacts as `PhysicsCollision` events (pre-step contact-point normal speed, time since the pair last touched, both sides' entity/surfaceprop). `Impacts::physics_collision_sounds` follows CBaseEntity::VPhysicsCollision -> PhysCollisionSound (>= 70 u/s, >= 0.05 s, volume (speed/320)^2) -> AddImpactSound (one slot per surfaceprop, merge past four, loudest origin) -> PlayImpactSounds (impactsoft when the hit surface's hardness is under this one's hard threshold or the speed under its hard velocity threshold); CWorld's VPhysicsCollision is empty so world sides stay silent; 'X' materials are silent. The host calls it after `physics.tick`; impact cues of every prop surfaceprop are preloaded.
- **Frag as a rigid body (owner report: no rolling, falls straight down off walls):** CGrenadeFrag::CreateVPhysics is VPhysicsInitNormal, i.e. a VPhysics object from w_grenade.phy. The host loads that .phy (`models::append_model_physics`); the frag then becomes a Rapier body (authored pieces or a 4-unit box, solid mass/damping/surfaceprop) in a COLLISION_GROUP_WEAPON-like group that touches world and props but not NPC colliders, and that player movement and weapon traces ignore. Each tick its pose/velocity/spin are read back and VPhysicsUpdate's 0.2 reflection off characters (line trace over this tick's travel) is applied; impact sounds come from the collision events. Without the .phy (unit tests, missing file) the fitted swept-box path stays. **The swept-box fit matched native within 1-22 units; the rigid body is not fitted** (restitution from elasticity product, Rapier friction, no rolling-resistance term): the local session must compare both against the recorded native samples before choosing.
- **+USE carry (owner request: E picks up small props and crates):** `player_pickup.rs` = CPlayerPickupController + CHL2_Player::PickupObject + CanPickupObject(35 kg, 128 units) + FindUseEntity (1024-unit line, then 16-unit hulls over 72 units at the SDK down tangents, first usable prop within PLAYER_USE_RADIUS 80): not what you stand on, the weapon must holster (holster hooks run, viewmodel and its effects hidden), PhysicsImpactSound feedback (the prop's impact sound vs "player" at 64 u/s), CGrabController with ignore-pitch and 30-degree AlignAngles, held attack throws (player_throwforce 1000 x RemapVal(clamp(mass, 0.5, 15), 0.5, 15, 0.5, 4) as an impulse, random torque approximated), held attack2 / E again / error > 12 drop (velocity cleared when touching, else clamped), then the weapon redeploys. Sprint is disabled while carrying. E falls through to doors/buttons when no prop is found.
- **Shared grab controller:** `grab.rs` is the gravity gun's CGrabController, now shared. **Behavior change for the gravity gun:** UpdateObject computes `forward` before the +/-75 pitch clamp (a dead store in the SDK), so the hold target is no longer pitch-clamped (the previous cloud port clamped it). Rotational damping 10 while held and restored afterwards (AttachEntity). Not modeled: REDUCED_CARRY_MASS (mass 1 while held), EnableDrag(false), preferred carry angles, the shadow controller itself ("native fit needed").
- **Changed existing behavior to check:** unauthored props now meet world brushes with friction 0.7 x 0.8 (product rule) instead of Rapier's average; all dynamic bodies get the air drag; NPC model colliders are in their own collision group.
- **Not done:** player-prop interaction (walking into props does not push them: there is no player VPhysics shadow), FVPHYSICS_NO_PLAYER_PICKUP/hinges, PhysCollisionScreenShake/dust, break sounds, scrape sounds, ragdolls/multi-solid geometry, damage from physics impacts (props hurting NPCs/player), prop_data/propdata.txt (health, breakables), motion-disabled props waking on physcannon, world per-brush materials.

Tests (Linux, at the merge): `cargo test -p hl2-simulation -p source-assets -p modkit-core -p hl2-ui -p hl2-bevy --locked` -> hl2-simulation 202 passed / 18 ignored, source-assets 92/19, modkit-core 53/0, hl2-ui 32/1, hl2-bevy 37/2; fmt clean; `CARGO_TERM_COLOR=never cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exit 0. Windows **not tested**.

### Update 2026-10-12 (same cloud session, continued): surface properties

Same agent/model and limits (Linux, no installation, no native HL2). The owner uploaded their installation's `scripts/surfaceproperties.txt` and `surfaceproperties_hl2.txt` into the session. They were used only for a temporary, uncommitted parser check (not committed; no game file is in the repository): 81 + 10 entries resolve into 91 materials with the expected inheritance, e.g. metalvehicle (base metal, defined in the other file), audioHardMinVelocity 500; gravel friction 0.4 with rock sounds; mixed-case keys, a missing space between two quoted tokens and trailing comments parse. The Valve wiki page returned HTTP 403 from the sandbox, so the SDK was used instead.
- 58aac27 **ground surface movement** (CGameMovement::CategorizeGroundSurface): grounded friction and acceleration use min(1.25 x surfaceprop friction, 1) (0.8 is normal: gravel 0.5, glass 0.625, mud 0.75, ice/slipperymetal 0.125), max speed x game.maxSpeedFactor, jump x game.jumpFactor. The host looks up the surfaceprop under the feet before each grounded step (the footstep lookup, `Gameplay::ground_surface`); in the air or without a surface table every factor is 1. `surfaceprops` also parses jumpfactor, maxspeedfactor, thickness and dampening (the last two are stored only: vphysics' use is not in the SDK). Restitution is clamped to 0..1 (metal_bouncy 1000, quicksand 2: IVP meaning unknown; Rapier above 1 adds energy). Finding: with slipperyslime's jumpfactor 0.7 the jump starts at 112 u/s, under NON_JUMP_VELOCITY 140, and the end-of-tick 2-unit ground test re-grounds the player in our model; check whether retail can jump off slipperyslime at all. Differences: the SDK keeps the last ground's speed factor in the air (ours resets to 1; every HL2 maxspeedfactor is 1); the lookup is one center ray, not the hull trace.
- 7245547 **per-side world materials**: VBSP gives each world collision triangle its brush side's $surfaceprop (utils/vbsp/ivp.cpp ConvertWorldBrushesToPhysCollide). The BSP reader stores each world brush side's material (`World::brush_side_materials`), the loader resolves their $surfaceprop once (`World::material_surfaceprops`), and brush colliders with any non-default side get a Rapier contact-modification hook (`WorldSideMaterials`) that uses the friction/restitution of the side facing the other body (product with the other collider). All-default brushes are unchanged. Not covered: brush entities (func_*), displacements/terrain (still "default"), and the player (the player is not a Rapier body; its friction comes from the ground-surface lookup above).

Tests (Linux, at 7245547): hl2-simulation 203 passed / 18 ignored, hl2-bevy 37/2, hl2-ui 32/1, modkit-core 54, source-assets 92/19; strict Clippy exit 0; fmt clean.

LOCAL VALIDATION CHECKLIST additions:

8. Player on low-friction ground: find a gravel/glass/metal-grate floor (e.g. d1_canals); walk, stop and measure the stopping distance against native (`cl_showpos 1`, same eye, release W at a marked point). Expected from the SDK: gravel stops in about twice the default distance.
9. Props on mixed floors: slide the same can across concrete and across a metal grate or ice texture, native vs ours.

Claude Code cloud session (Linux sandbox, no game files, no native HL2), model `claude-opus-5-5` per the session environment. No subagents. Branch from `origin/wip/arsenal-20261009` (b739e69). **Everything here is SDK 2013 (b8cfb12) behavior, unit-tested only; nothing was compared with retail or native, nothing packaged was run.** Write "not tested (cloud: no owned files/native)" for every visual/audio/timing claim.

Implemented (commits on this branch, newest last; see MODLOG):
- **weapon_crossbow** (`hl2-simulation/src/weapon_crossbow.rs`, thin hooks in gameplay.rs): fire (clip 1, 0.75 s), m_bMustReload reload once the fire sequence's idle time passes (strict), the base empty-click path, zoom on the attack2 *press* in post and busy frames (CBasePlayer::SetFOV 20 over 0.1 s, 0 over 0.2 s; GetFOV SimpleSpline ramp from the integer start), holster StopEffects, charger states from viewmodel events 3005/3013/3016 (START_LOAD plays SPECIAL1), skin glow/normal, HEV_AMO0 suit update. crossbow_bolt: MOVETYPE_FLYGRAVITY (gravity 0.05, half before/after the move), 0.3 box, BubbleThink angles every 0.1 s; world hit: BoltHitWorld, reflect at 0.75 speed with gravity 1.0 when hitDot < 0.5 and speed > 100, else stick (UTIL_ImpactTrace -> decal/impact sound through the host impact system, a never-dying stuck-bolt record 8 units back, glow sprite fading over 3 s, sparks event); damageable entities (NPC, breakable, physics prop): sk_plr_dmg_crossbow through the shared entity damage path, CalculateMeleeDamageForce-style impulse, BoltHitBody, stuck bolt on the world within 128 units behind; other entities: impact + removal.
- **weapon_rpg** (`weapon_rpg.rs`): one missile at a time, 0.5 s, muzzle eye + 12 fwd + 6 right - 3 up, launch velocity fwd x 300 + 128 up, 0.3 s grace if the first 128 units are clear, ammo decrement, laser starts after the draw (StartGuiding SPECIAL1), dot suppressed during ACT_VM_RELOAD, WeaponIdle only with no buttons held (so holding attack after a reload keeps ACT_VM_RELOAD and blocks firing, as the SDK), holster refused with a missile out (StopGuiding SPECIAL2 otherwise), NotifyRocketDied -> reload sound + ACT_VM_RELOAD. env_laserdot: eye trace (56755.84), damageable target entity, LaserThink scale every 0.05 s. rpg_missile: FLYGRAVITY until IgniteThink at 0.3 s (w_missile_launch -> w_missile, Missile.Ignite, white 64-alpha 0.1 s FFADE_IN), then MOVETYPE_FLY at 1500 with SeekThink every tick (RPG_HOMING_SPEED 0.125 blend; ComputeActualDotPosition line-following within 512 units / nearer than the dot, direct chase of targeted entities); explosion 200 damage / 200 radius through the shared blast (player self-damage included). RocketTrail puffs (SDK parameters) drawn as quads.
- **weapon_bugbait** (`weapon_bugbait.rs`): attack haul back (ACT_VM_HAULBACK), throw on release after the drawback event 3900 (ACT_VM_THROW), launch at event 3005 (eye + 18 fwd + 12 right, player velocity + 1000 fwd, spin 600/RandomInt(-1200,1200), 0.1 s grace when clear), redraw after the throw sequence, squeeze on attack2 (Weapon_Bugbait.Splat, ACT_VM_SECONDARYATTACK). No ammo. npc_grenade_bugbait: FLYGRAVITY (600), 2-unit box, splat on first solid contact (GrenadeBugBait.Splat). point_bugbait sensors (radius, Enabled, SF 1 suppress call / 2 not thrown / 4 not squeezed) fire OnBaited; `BugBaitEvent` (position, squeezed, call, combine soldiers within 150 with a clear line) is the step-14 hook (cleared each tick by the host until antlions exist). SDK bait is MOVETYPE_FLYGRAVITY, not VPhysics, so no drag/surfaceprop contact applies. Not done: SporeExplosion particles, BeerSplash decal, HitByBugbait input delivery, an Enable input after "Enabled" "0".
- **weapon_physcannon** (`weapon_physcannon.rs`) on the existing Rapier prop bodies: punt with attack (8-unit hull, then line, 250 units; PuntVPhysics impulses 15000 kg in/s at the center + min(mass, 250) x 600 at the hit point; downward aim z x -0.65; PrimaryFireEffect white 32-alpha flash + SINGLE; non-VPhysics breakables take 1 DMG_CRUSH; NPCs dry-fire), pull/pickup with attack2 (FindObjectTrace line then 4-unit hull to 1000 units: attach within 250, pull beyond with 4000 x (mass + 0.5)/50 under 50 kg; FindObjectInCone 0.97 within 251), deny (SPECIAL3 once per press) above physcannon_maxmass 250, hold (CGrabController::UpdateObject target: 24 + 2r forward with r = player radius + object extent along the aim, pitch clamped to +/-75, brush trace pull-in, pushed off the player axis, eye-space rotation kept; velocity toward the target capped 1000 u/s / 3600 deg/s, gravity off; error accumulator detaches past 12 after 1 s), drop on the attack2 press (velocity clamped to 285 u/s, 720 deg/s, MELEE_MISS), launch with attack (Pickup_DefaultPhysGunLaunchVelocity 1500 up to 100 kg, spline to 700 at 600 kg), element open/close (SPECIAL2/MELEE_HIT, "active" pose value), idle shake while holding, holster refused while holding.
- Gravity gun limits: our props get Rapier mass 10 per collider, **not the authored .phy/$mass**, so mass-dependent results (punt speed, deny threshold, launch spline) differ from native until the local session wires real masses; the shadow controller is a velocity approximation (VPhysics internals, "native fit needed"); no VPhysics drag on punted props (frag fit c = 6.4e-4 is a candidate, "native fit needed"); no surfaceprop impact sounds for props yet (reuse the frag's PhysCollisionSound helper); motor loop sound, beams/glow sprites, view punch, the random launch spin, player speed while carrying, ground-entity and hinge/NO_PLAYER_PICKUP checks, ragdoll punts and the mega cannon are not done. `player_throwforce` belongs to the +USE carry throw (CPlayerPickupController), not the gravity gun, so it is not used here.
- Host wiring (hl2-bevy, **compiles on Linux, never run**): bolt/missile models, cue preloads (Weapon_Crossbow.BoltHitWorld/BoltHitBody, Missile.Ignite; script SoundData is preloaded as before), attack2 routed to the crossbow, `inventory.fov.default_fov` + `view_fov` drive the camera FOV, projectile impacts drained into the impact system, weapon suit updates forwarded, laser dot copied to projectiles each tick, missile events back to the RPG, new viewmodels load sequences by activity (ACT_* placeholders resolved on the next tick).

SDK vs owner-prompt differences (decide locally; "retail check needed"):
- The SDK crossbow does **not** unzoom on reload (DefaultReload never calls SetFOV; zoom can even toggle during reload busy frames). Implemented per SDK; unzoom only on holster.
- The SDK RPG has **no attack2 laser toggle in singleplayer** ("Players are not allowed to remove the laser guide in single player"). Implemented per SDK.
- The player missile's damage is CMissile::Spawn's hard-coded 200; `sk_plr_dmg_rpg_round` is only the ammo type's cvar. Implemented 200.
- The crossbow bolt's SpriteTrail is commented out in the SDK, and the missile trail is a RocketTrail particle emitter, so `sprite_trail` was not needed for either.
- Bolt water speed (1500) is unused: no player water level yet.

Not done / known gaps: stuck bolts are recorded (`projectiles.stuck_bolts`, report) but **not drawn**; sparks event not drawn; charger/load sprites, laser beam and muzzle sprite are state only (no host drawing); viewmodel skin not applied; ragdoll pinning; sky surfaces are not distinguished (bolts stick to / missiles explode on sky brushes); Missile.Ignite is never stopped (game cues have no stop path); ViewPunch, SOUND_COMBAT/danger sounds (AI step 14); RPG lowered idle when out of ammo; ExplosionCreate's env_explosion origin adjustments (shared blast uses position + 1 up).

## Current session 11 (2026-10-10)

Supersedes session 10's arsenal notes where they conflict. Claude Code desktop, model `claude-opus-5-5`. No subagents. Weekly usage was 92-94% (resets 2026-10-12 16:00 UTC), so the session was short.

- **Frag vs native (13b, `wip/arsenal-20261009`):** quadratic air drag fitted to native ent_text positions (c = 6.4e-4/unit; packaged run within 7-20 units of native over 1.3 s; rest position within 4 units), SDK spin range fixed, trail drawn as one C_SpriteTrail strip with life-based alpha/width. Native oracle supports per-view `!attack`/`!release`/`!attack2`/`!release2`/`!duck`/`!unduck`/`!text` suffixes (private).
- **Lob/roll vs native (later in session 11):** frag world contacts are VPhysics-like (surfaceprop friction impulse, no bounce, edge sliding, 190 u/s^2 rolling resistance). Roll (secondary while crouched) RMS 22 units over 2.2 s; lob and overhand flights/landings within 1-11 units; after landing the lob and the overhand throw diverge (51-151 units) because spin-to-roll needs rotational dynamics (a Rapier body from w_grenade.phy is the next option).
- **Regression batch (0e41ccb):** artifacts/arsenal-regression 26/17/26, final captures unchanged vs artifacts/damage-regression apart from known slate exposure/book-edge noise. Rerun before the 13b merge.
- **Rejected (2 attempts): native oracle at host_timescale 0.05** (fuse-timing capture, sessions view-d1_trainstation_02-20261010T043102Z/T043310Z): the client overflows the user command buffer ("User command buffer overflow", 224/307 warnings, visible in the oracle window) and drops input, so +attack never threw. Timescale 0.1 runs logged no overflow; done at 0.1 (T043539Z): native detonates 2.94-3.15 s after spawn, ours 3.045 s. Native post-landing rest positions vary ~28 units run to run (random spin).
- **Frag impact sounds:** Grenade.ImpactHard per SDK PhysCollisionSound (>= 70 u/s, (speed/320)^2); packaged trace confirms the owned wave; not compared with native audio.
- **Found, not fixed:** native Sprite materials blend in gamma space (`$nosrgb` default 1, TONEMAP_SCALE_GAMMA) into the integer-HDR framebuffer; ours blend in linear space, so additive sprites (trail, glows, muzzle flashes, combine ball) are dimmer than native. Needs a renderer design (DESIGN 11f).
- **Cloud session prepared (owner, 2026-10-10):** a cloud prompt (private work/publishing/cloud-session-prompt-20261010.txt) assigns SDK-only, unit-tested work on `wip/cloud-arsenal-20261010` (crossbow, RPG, bug bait, gravity gun, from this branch) and `wip/cloud-npc-ai-20261010` (step 14 AI core, F4 spawn data model, from main). The next local session validates those branches against the owned game and native HL2 before anything merges.

Claude Code cloud session (Linux sandbox, no game files, no native HL2), model `claude-opus-5-5` per the session environment. No subagents. Branch from `origin/main` (31e9134), independent of the arsenal branch (see `wip/cloud-arsenal-20261010` for 13b). **SDK 2013 (b8cfb12) behavior, unit-tested only; retail not compared, nothing packaged was run.**

Implemented in `hl2-simulation/src/ai/` (plan: DESIGN step 14 "14 plan"):
- `conditions`: COND_* bitset with the SDK names/order (ai_condition.h), class-specific bits after LAST_SHARED_CONDITION.
- `state`: NPC_STATE and SelectIdealState (idle/alert/combat/script selectors, TIME_CARE_ABOUT_DAMAGE 3 s, the yaw target for damage/sounds).
- `memory`: CAI_Enemies (UpdateMemory reacquire rules, free knowledge 1.75 s, discard 60 s, danger memories).
- `relationships`: Class_T and the InitDefaultAIRelationships table (676 rows generated from the SDK source with comments removed); Classify for the main HL2 classes (citizens passive while gordon_precriminal/citizens_passive).
- `default_schedules` + `schedule`: the ai_default.cpp schedule table as data (all 86 AI_DEFINE_SCHEDULE definitions, generated from the SDK), a parser for the SDK "Tasks/Interrupts" text format (for class schedules such as npc_metropolice's), MaintainSchedule (interrupt masks, COND_TASK_FAILED -> SelectFailSchedule/m_failSchedule, COND_SCHEDULE_DONE, TASK_SET_SCHEDULE, up to 8 instant tasks per think), ~40 generic tasks through a host `Motor` trait (activity, stop, face, path to enemy/target/last position/sound, wait for movement, play sequence/attacks), unknown tasks fail and are counted, a class `Behavior` trait (SelectSchedule/TranslateSchedule/SelectFailSchedule/StartTask/RunTask) and the base SelectSchedule (without flinch/interactions/weapons/shot regulator).
- `senses`: CSoundEnt (types, volume radius, expiry, 64 sounds), Listen -> COND_HEAR_*/SMELL/NO_HEAR_DANGER and a best sound (danger first, then nearest: simplified GetBestSound), Look with the 2D FInViewCone dot, 2048 look distance and a host `SightWorld` LOS trait -> SEE_PLAYER/HATE/FEAR, best enemy by priority then distance.
- `squad`: CAI_Squad per-enemy strategy slots (PER_ENEMY_SQUADSLOTS), leader, members for shared memory, SquadNewEnemy rule.
- `spawn`: npc_create / npc_create_aimed / npc_create_equipment parsing and CC_NPC_Create placement; the F4 overlay model (class and weapon lists are developer choices).
- `police`: ai_goal_police + CAI_PolicingBehavior for the trainstation posts: COND_POLICE_TARGET_TOO_CLOSE_HARASS (within 2x PoliceRadius, 32 units of height) / SUPPRESS (inside the radius, or a visible target behind the post with the knockout flag or EnableKnockOut), MOVE_ALONG_A/B/C warnings every 4-6 s (TASK_POLICE_ANNOUNCE_HARASS) with HARASS1/HARASS2 gestures, OnFirstWarning/OnSecondWarning/OnLastWarning/OnSupressingTarget outputs (returned for the host to fire), baton on the last warning, hostility (enemy, combat state) after POLICE_MAX_WARNINGS 4 or on SUPPRESS, return to post (16 units), face along the goal; the six policing schedules in the SDK text format. Not done: the npc_metropolice class itself (its own schedules, shove ACT_PUSH_PLAYER / IncrementPlayerCriminalStatus / AdministerJustice / METROPOLICE_MAX_WARNINGS 3 on the class, stunstick melee, pistol/SMG combat, manhacks), npc_citizen and npc_combine_s.
- (Superseded below: host wiring, npc_metropolice and the F4 overlay landed later the same day.)

### Update 2026-10-10 (later, third cloud session): step 14 (1)-(5) wired (647a982, dd0073f, +USE follow-up)

Same agent/model and limits as above (Linux, no game files, no native HL2; **SDK behavior, unit-tested only, nothing packaged was run, retail not compared**). `origin/main` has since moved to dedeb19 (README only); this branch was not rebased.
- `ai::host`: `SceneMotor` implements `Motor` over the npc.rs `Controller` (AI goals use the reserved `AI_SCENE` GoalKey scene with a per-runtime serial; released on stop/new path) and the scene's actor animation (`Scene::ai_set_activity`, `ai_activity_finished`, `ai_set_yaw`, `ai_events`); yaw turns at MaxYawSpeed x 10 deg/s with the 0.2 s UpdateYaw clamp and FacingIdeal 0.006 degrees. `PhysicsSight` implements `SightWorld` (MASK_BLOCKLOS: world/brush/prop hits block, `npc_*` bodies are skipped). `PhysicsPlacement` implements CC_NPC_Create placement (HULL_HUMAN for every class; per-class hulls not modeled).
- `ai::runtime::AiRuntime`: the NPCThink loop (0.1 s, no PVS/efficiency throttling or staggering), GatherConditions (sight of the player/hated NPCs, hearing, enemy memory, NEW_ENEMY/SEE_ENEMY/ENEMY_OCCLUDED/LOST_ENEMY), SelectIdealState and MaintainSchedule through the class behavior. **Off by default**: `--ai` on the command line or the console `ai_enable [0|1]` (not a cheat; our command, the SDK's ai_disable is the inverse). NPCs owned by a scripted_sequence or a choreographed scene are NPC_STATE_SCRIPT and the AI yields (cancels its goal, counts `scripted_skips`).
- `ai::metropolice`: npc_metropolice from npc_metropolice.cpp for the precriminal campaign: criminal status/relationship (precriminal player is neutral unless chasing), look-at-near-player through `LookTargets`, baton (ACT_ACTIVATE_BATON/DEACTIVATE), shove (ACT_PUSH_PLAYER, IncrementPlayerCriminalStatus, METROPOLICE_MAX_WARNINGS 3, then ACT_MELEE_ATTACK1), AdministerJustice / allowed-to-respond (SF 0x01000000), prechase return, the thrown-physics-object reaction and the stunstick melee (32-unit swing hull, sk_npc_dmg_stunstick, punch/push, red fade or white knock-out fade when the police goal says knock out); shove/stunstick damage is DMG_CLUB, precriminal players take no NPC melee damage. Class schedules in the SDK text format; custom interrupts (BuildScheduleTestBits). `ai::police` is hosted when the cop receives `SetPoliceGoal <goal>` (the SDK input; the ai_goal_police entity is a plain CBaseEntity with no actor keyvalue or Activate); ai_goal_police `EnableKnockOut/DisableKnockOut` and its OnKnockOut/On*Warning/OnSupressingTarget outputs are delivered through the scene. Sentences (METROPOLICE_*) play positionally with NPC_Metropolice.SentenceParameters.
- +USE on a cop with the AI on goes to PrecriminalUse (SetUse in Spawn replaces the ordinary use: warnings 1-3, then AdministerJustice); with the AI off the ordinary use is unchanged.
- npc_metropolice is registered as AI-only ground actor: choreographed scene requests for it report MissingActor (scripted_sequence still owns it).
- Host (`hl2-bevy`): the AI tick runs in the NPC stage before `tick_npcs`; player hits apply damage, impulse (`player_impulse`, before the movement step) and screen fades; report keys `ai` and `npc_spawns`.
- F4 overlay (ours; avoids native F5/F6/F9/F3): F4 toggles, Left/Right class, Up/Down weapon, Enter places at the crosshair like npc_create_aimed; lines drawn with the F1 debug text (outside the claimed hl2-ui menus). Console `npc_create`/`npc_create_aimed` (sv_cheats) and `npc_create_equipment` route to `ai::spawn::Console`. **Runtime NPC creation is not implemented**: World/Scene entity tables are fixed at map load (states, actor registration, models, physics colliders all index the load-time entity list), so a spawn request only computes the CC_NPC_Create placement and logs it (console + report `npc_spawns`). DESIGN step 14 ("14 status") records the plan.
- Not done: npc_citizen and npc_combine_s classes; view punch from hits (the punch path lives on the arsenal branch); `hit_by_player_thrown_object` host call (needs the physics/physcannon branches' carry/launch events); CSoundEnt insertion from gameplay (gunfire, footsteps, impacts: only the runtime API exists, so hearing gets no player sounds); transient NPC hulls are passed as `&[]` on AI paths; ACT_IDLE_ANGRY and other activity translations not mapped; pistol/SMG combat, manhacks, arrests.

Update 2026-10-12 (9890103): **F4 and npc_create now place real NPCs.** Runtime entity creation still does not exist, so the map loader appends a hidden spawn pool: `ai::spawn::append_pool` adds 3 entities per overlay class (`hl2rs_spawn_pool` key; origin 0 0 -15000; SDK default models from each class's Spawn/Precache: police, combine_soldier, humans/group01/male_07, barney, alyx, vortigaunt, zombie/classic, headcrabclassic, antlion, manhack, combine_scanner) before `append_models`, so models, animation, physics colliders and AI actors are built like map NPCs. Pooled entities start invisible (no collision, no AI think). A spawn takes the next free slot of the class, moves it to the CC_NPC_Create placement, sets the yaw and shows it; a cop's baton follows the chosen equipment (`AiRuntime::set_equipment`). When a class's 3 slots are used the console says so. The F4 overlay shows the last result. Limits: 3 per class per map load; no weapon models in NPC hands; only npc_metropolice has AI (others stand in their default animation); console names (`npc_create <class> <name>`) are not applied (the World is shared and immutable); the extra models load at map start (load time to check). Test: `a_pooled_cop_is_hidden_until_placed_then_thinks`, `the_pool_has_slots_for_every_overlay_class`. Later the same day (see the next commit): the roster grew to 41 HL2 campaign NPC classes with SDK models (adds dog, kleiner, eli, mossman, breen, gman, monk, zombie_torso, fastzombie, poisonzombie, the fast/black/poison headcrabs, barnacle, antlionguard, ichthyosaur, crow, pigeon, seagull, stalker, clawscanner, rollermine, floor/ceiling turrets, combine_camera, sniper, strider, gunship, helicopter, dropship; helpers, makers, grenades and episodic-only classes excluded). Strider, gunship, helicopter, dropship, antlion guard and ichthyosaur get 1 slot, the rest 3: 111 pooled entities per map. The overlay is a scrolling list (9 rows, Up/Down class, Left/Right weapon, the weapon choices listed with the selection in brackets). Placement still uses HULL_HUMAN for every class (large NPCs may be accepted or refused wrongly); fliers back off 36 units. Load time with ~30 extra models must be measured locally; if too slow, lower `pool_count`. F4 debug switches (overlay open): I = AI on/off (off drops every AI NPC's schedule/enemy/movement goal and returns it to ACT_IDLE, so nothing fights), O = AI ignores the player (like ai_ignoreplayers/notarget: the player is not sensed, NPCs still sense the world and each other), P = stance of every AI NPC toward the player: default (table/campaign rules) -> hate -> neutral -> like; the overlay shows the three states. Only npc_metropolice has AI, so NPC-vs-NPC fights and per-class stances matter once more classes get AI; console equivalents (ai_disable, ai_ignoreplayers, notarget) are not added. Owner decision 2026-10-12: runtime entity creation replaces the pool; plan DESIGN "14r" (progress there: step 1 store/spawn_entity, step 2 runtime lookups safe for spawned ids, step 3 AiRuntime::add_actor; next: npc Controller registration, physics colliders, async late precache, Bevy instancing, then remove the pool; ca1a40b was pushed failing strict Clippy (match_like_matches/single-arm match) and fixed in the next commit); AGENTS.md now allows runtime loads through asynchronous workers. Regression note: entity counts grow by 111 per map (ids of map entities are unchanged); owned scripts that count entities must be checked.

## Current session 8 (2026-10-08)

Supersedes session 7's open items below where they conflict. Claude Code desktop, model `claude-opus-5-5`.

- **Weapon-switch tick: fixed.** The owner narrowed it to the slot-move tick (`wpn_moveselect.wav`, 8-bit 22 kHz). It was audible in scripted runs too. Process recordings showed the attack missing for ~12 ms. Cause: rodio 0.22 players start with a mono 48 kHz filler span, and the first 512 samples of any other format are resampled as 48 kHz mono. All waves are now converted to 48 kHz at load; stereo one-shots get one silent span first. Burst levels match the file within 0.1 dB, and the owner confirmed by ear. Method: docs/validation.md "Audio verification method". New `--audio-trace`, `--mute-ambient`.

## Goal

Faithful Rust reconstruction of Half-Life 2 and relevant Source behavior using assets from an owned installation. This is a rewrite, not a passthrough mod. Full 1:1 behavior and ordinary first-level campaign completion remain unfinished. No FAL or crossovers.

## Setup

- Owned Steam HL2 app220, tested build19307283/patch9912070; other versions **not tested**. Default install `C:/Program Files (x86)/Steam/steamapps/common/Half-Life 2`, or discovered library/HL2_ROOT.
- Windows11 Pro64-bit, Rust1.99.0 MSVC, Visual Studio C++ tools/Windows SDK; Bevy0.19.1/wgpu and direct Rapier0.26.1. No Source DLL or mod loader dependency.
- macOS 15+ aarch64 (Apple Silicon), Rust stable, Bevy 0.19.1/wgpu (Metal backend), cpal/CoreAudio, winit/AppKit: tested by a contributor (@OrbitalMan, PR #1: packaged build on an M1 Pro running d1_trainstation_02 with its scripted sequences). Windows remains the primary tested platform; replays, captures and native comparisons in this file are Windows-only.
- Implementation chats: Claude Code desktop (Claude Opus 5.5) since 2026-10-06; earlier Codex Desktop, GPT-6.1 Extra High/Normal Speed. Choose fresh-chat settings explicitly.
- Active checkout: workspace `outputs/hl2-rs-bevy`, branch `main` (Bevy-only since the 2026-10-07 merge), remote https://github.com/kvalls/hl2-rs. `bevy-migration` is frozen at the merge. ## What works (tested)

- Shared bounded VPK/BSP/material/model/animation/audio readers, owned PHY convex pieces, fixed15ms movement including air crouch, synchronized door/collider poses and limited Rapier props.
- Six primary weapons, shotgun one-shell secondary fallback, SMG contact grenades/AR2 charge and ball behavior, selection/ammo/HUD animation, pause/console subset and actual Bevy audio sinks. Controlled fixtures test these; the full arsenal is not implemented.
- Bevy host consumes shared gameplay/entity state and presents sky, GPU skeletal animation, animated iris projection, authored timed LOOKAT interests, impacts/partial particles, live camera monitors and landmark/inventory transitions. General AI and scenes remain partial.
- Native owned1080 captures establish white five-dot22x22 reticle and yellow pistol/SMG21x17. Seven Bevy720/1080/borderless captures and retained1080 captures match dot offsets. Owned Windows GDI setup fixes blur/fractional positioning; color/tone/general-font parity is not claimed.
- launch-bevy-1080p.cmd and launch-bevy-borderless.cmd both captured1920x1080. The former Macroquad launchers/build script were removed with that host.
- GPU skinning, player/monitor BSP PVS and shared materials improve measured local performance. Optional --world-partition splits opaque/cutout world batches; inspected plaza/spawn images are identical on/off. It stays off by default.
- Shared Source layer composition (delta/post layers, bone masks, autolayer ramps) and scene GESTURE execution: tag retiming, intensity, posture suppression and end/cancel fades. Both hosts compose base clip and layers. Barney, Kleiner and the G-Man actor layer their authored security/broadcast gestures in packaged Bevy runs. Not yet compared with the original game.
- point_template children spawn on ForceSpawn (Barney appears when the arrival trigger fires); motion-disabled and start-asleep prop_physics flags; NPC routes open unlocked doors.
- Latest runtime checks (2026-10-06, templates/doors): 308 normal and 21 owned-install tests, strict Clippy/fmt, packaged regression batch (see docs/validation.md). Earlier gesture checks: 307 normal tests, 21 owned-install checks, strict workspace all-target Clippy/fmt; 1,600-tick packaged security timeline; regression captures identical to 28c0bb3 apart from one recorded nondeterministic Breen variation that did not repeat; movement 26, weapons/doors 17 and attention 26 assertions; retained smoke capture. See docs/validation.md.

## What doesn't work yet

- General NPC schedules/navigation/combat and full first-level camera/train/Breen/Kleiner staging; ordinary campaign completion.
- Layered gestures/autolayers/delta-post composition, weighted blends, head poses, facial flexes/lip sync/IK, ragdolls and full studio events.
- Remaining weapons, exact spread/prediction, complete damage/death, vehicles, save/load and complete entity/global/player transfer.
- Exact Havok/VPhysics dynamics, animated collision, moving platforms and blocked-door handling. Rapier/Avian comparative performance is **not tested**; a physics migration was not requested.
- Source HDR/fog/shadows/refraction/material stages, area portals/occluders/LODs, recursive camera/proxy fidelity, complete particle effects/DSP/soundscapes and VGUI/console parity.
- Station03 has25 known unsupported-material errors, unchanged in the latest campaign fixture. Native HL2 skips d2_coast_02, moving from d2_coast_01 to d2_coast_03 (owner confirmed 2026-10-16, including native console-load failure). The local zero-byte stub is an expected rejection, excluded from campaign work; no reconstruction is needed.
- Platforms other than Windows and contributor-tested macOS Apple Silicon, native/campaign-wide144FPS and complete pixel parity are **not tested** or established.

## Which game owns the player

Not applicable: this is a rewrite. Rust simulation owns player/gameplay state. The installed original supplies content and is run separately as a comparison oracle.

## The current problem

Saved analysis snapshots cover 42 selected modules and 122,721 entries with some pseudocode. Read-only discovery adds 10,752 separate temporary entries, including 8,992 callback bodies and 62 bounded-disassembly bodies in this continuation. The consolidated index audits 133,473 distinct observed addresses, 133,556 export variants, 51,823 candidate/control rows and 13 immutable source datasets. All 16 original failures and their relaxed-input recoveries remain preserved; 39 failed variants include duplicates and rejected experiments. The original 50-file/633,910,865-byte project and source databases remain unchanged. These counts are an observed corpus, not a proven full-game denominator. Scope, names, ABI, shared/indirect flow and native behavior remain unverified and **not tested** against gameplay.

Pass08 verified rollback for all 2,462 inspected addresses:62 exports, 2,333 typed/overlapping-data exclusions and 67 unresolved flow cases. Pass11 matches all 2,333 data units against private PE bytes, including 2,074 relocated words:2,030 reach existing functions and 44 reach unowned executable storage. Ten switch controls and 34 bounded nearby-entry controls add variants on existing entries, with unchanged metadata. One server control records76 decompiler instruction addresses outside its listing body; one of46 orphan dispatcher/seed cases has an observed switch relationship and45 remain unmatched. Listing gaps, decompiler coverage and new entries are different measures. Do not create one function per switch case.

The final callback C-only navigator indexes8,945/8,992 bodies, with47 omissions and48 range mismatches; the disassembly navigator indexes62/62 with zero discrepancies. Mixed C/TXT indexing produced929 false rows, and an incomplete-copy index was rejected. Exact path/hash/address/source/variant retrieval is authoritative and refuses ambiguous output. Reviewed names/types, Rust implementation and native verification tables stay empty in this research index.

Discovery on the 22-module clone leaves 1,172,660 executable bytes outside functions and 1,488 unowned pointer-slot observations, down from the original 1,459,199 bytes/13,830 slots. The 17 optional modules add 385,203 gap bytes/3,390 observations; their saved entry counts exactly match export snapshots. A Vulkan shader exception-handler analyzer error remains recorded despite successful exports. Database audits preserve 20,842 discovery artifacts/9,850 bodies, 393 optional artifacts/23,987 bodies plus five original error rows, and the 34/11 temporary body ledgers; all have zero discrepancies. The discovery navigator indexes all 9,850 bodies with one range mismatch; the optional navigator misses 2,544 bodies and has 779 mismatches. Use exact path/hash/address retrieval as authority.

The following paragraphs retain the initial coverage-pass-20261005-01 baseline, before this continuation:

The immediate priority is trustworthy function discovery and remaining runtime scope. All 11 known failures now also have separately saved supplemental bodies after removing conflicting analysis-inferred input parameters in rolled-back read-only Ghidra sessions. Original signatures were verified afterward. A return-type-only pilot failed and is retained. Successful export under relaxed metadata does not recover a trustworthy ABI or prove semantics; all 11 are **not tested** against native behavior. The 25-module scope and function denominator are not complete-game claims.

The 22-module discovery audit records 19,725,824 initialized executable bytes, 18,266,625 in identified function bodies and 1,459,199 outside them. Of those gaps, 545,133 are uniform INT3 bytes, 340 uniform zero bytes and 913,726 unclassified. There are 13,830 read-only pointer-slot observations targeting executable addresses outside functions and 133,588 computed-call instructions. The recorded direct-call target check found no unowned target, but that does not resolve indirect calls or prove boundaries. No candidates were automatically promoted. Recognized VPhysics vtable labels had zero unowned slots; remaining unlabelled tables/shared tails require evidence-based inspection.

The new path/hash/address supplement preserves 301 artifacts, 1,090 extension bodies, 11 recovery bodies and failed/rejected attempts; its audit has zero discrepancies. The original gamedb navigator's recorded 78,070 bodies/9,713 omissions/2,810 range mismatches were not re-audited here. A separate fresh extension navigator indexes 827 of 1,090 bodies, omits 263 and has 36 range mismatches after declared CRLF-to-LF comparison. Scoped CreateInterface retrieval is a positive control. Naming/type review, Rust implementation and native verification tables stay separate and empty in the supplement; no implementation was marked complete.

Active runtime work (session 3, 2026-10-06): polishing the security_02 Barney scene toward 1:1 (owner priority). Native close-ups at 14-degree FOV (oracle `ORACLE_CLOSEUP="pitch yaw fov"`; Bevy fixtures test-inputs/bevy-face-security-*.json with `pitch`/`fov`) now drive face work. Done this session: FACS eyelids (lids were half-closed; retail StudioRender 1001bd80); entity parenting with animated attachments, so helmetBack rides on Barney (faceplates are off by security_02 because ss_Helmet_Reveal runs in security_01); retained-host flexes and `--volume`; lip sync from WAV VDAT phonemes and VFE phoneme settings (retail client viseme path); head/body/gesture flexes in actor pose parameters using eyes/forward attachments; Combine camera deploy/retract activities; movement turning at MaxYawSpeed with move_yaw. The session 2 ~5-unit mark offset no longer reproduces (origin equals mark_barneyroom_monitor_3; silhouettes match within about one unit at 18.05 s). Open differences against native: (1) entity lighting: done 2026-10-07 (leaf ambient cubes + world lights per retail light cache; Barney close-ups and the trainstation_03 corridor match native tone). Shadows, bump/specular and HDR tonemapping remain. (2) Native head-down at 21-22.5 s: explained and fixed 2026-10-07. Template-spawned actors had no scene gesture clips in Bevy (rubNeck/thinking were dropped); close-ups now match native. (3) Brow intensity at 18.05 s looks stronger than native (lighting makes this hard to judge). (4) The reversed camera-open transition, camera aim and eye sprites are not modeled. (5) The retained host does not spawn Barney from his template in a security smoke run. (6) Script events now remove the faceplate (verified only by starting ss_Helmet_Reveal directly at Barney's corridor spawn, which is not the campaign staging). Owner-described campaign order: in the main room the other metrocop leaves ("need help with this one?") and the door closes; masked Barney says "I'm going to need some privacy for this", turns off the cameras, then security_01 fires ss_Helmet_Reveal ("about that beer I owed you", normal voice). That chain has not been played or compared with native. (7) Owner: godrays were overexposed. Fixed in Bevy by applying entity rendercolor (vol_light shafts are tinted to about 19%); native comparison 2026-10-07: shaft brightness comparable. The jumbotron's apparent see-through areas were Breen's missing studio backdrop (infodecal); fixed with static decals. The old Breen fixture looks edge-on at jumbotron1 at 7 s; use test-inputs/bevy-monitors-breen-screen.json (25 s, face-on). Known gaps: cross-scene layer priority, empty-name gesture handling, lip-sync voice time is scene time rather than mixer position.

Private database/native exports and published SDK research corroborate selected behavior. The entire game has **not** been decompiled and translated; indexing functions is not a completed rewrite. Keep byte/hash provenance, mark inferred signatures and keep native code/databases outside public Rust sources.

## Claims (shared work)

One list of priorities, with claims: an item someone takes gets a GitHub issue assigned to them (or an open PR) and is listed here with its owner. Unclaimed items are free. Before starting anything, check open issues and PRs (`gh issue list`, `gh pr list`) and this list; leave claimed items, and the files they touch, alone.

- Done: macOS support (install discovery, POSIX build/launch scripts), PR #1 by @OrbitalMan, merged 2026-10-07 (ad480e1) after Windows CI passed.

Keep other work out of the hl2-ui menu code and its hl2-bevy wiring meanwhile.
- HL2 UI and Options implementation (menus, dev console, graphics/gameplay options, guardrails): @OrbitalMan, [PR #4](https://github.com/kvalls/hl2-rs/pull/4) (opened 2026-10-26 against main, 100 commits, 377 files; it also splits hl2-simulation entities.rs/ai runtime.rs and rewrites hl2-ui console.rs). Session 32 kept new commands out of console.rs (F4 option instead).
- Save/load: deferred by the owner (2026-10-07) until entity/scene state settles; start with a design issue when it resumes.

Native oracle (private work/hl2-decompiled/original-oracle): run_view_oracle.py <map> label=x,y,eyez,pitch,yaw[,fov] and run_security_gesture_oracle.py. Owner-verified cursor-safe setup (2026-10-07): -nomouse (SDK Init_Mouse returns early) + +cl_mouseenable 0 + the game window disabled (EnableWindow FALSE, WS_EX_NOACTIVATE) on appearance + focus guard + ClipCursor release. Earlier runs trapped the cursor (sessions T040936Z rejected, T053505Z had 91 focus grabs).

## What we've already tried

- The VPhysics two-double-width input pilot at100977ad still fails with overlapping input varnodes. All16 original signatures/storage/body checks pass afterward. Fifteen failures share a normalized instruction shape; FileSystem_Stdio follows a distinct SSE path. Do not repeat the failed width correction across the corpus or adopt relaxed-input `void` prototypes as verified ABIs.
- Bounded orphan ownership tests consider at most two preceding entries within16KiB. All34 controls export, but45/46 dispatcher/seed cases remain unmatched. Stop nearby-entry guesses; next trace exact raw branches, table bounds/words and shared tails.

- Continuation discovery on a private clone identifies and exports 9,850 additional candidate entries, with no export failures. Boundaries require relocated pointer slots, defined instructions, coherent bounded flow and independent entry evidence. Original scope/semantics remain unverified. Raw RTTI validation initially rejected writable type descriptors; the rejected report is retained and initialized readable records are now checked against pinned Ghidra layouts.
- Callback discovery's first parallel exporter rejected its own inline rollback checks because GhidraScript still held an enclosing transaction. Do not repeat inline validation or count those partial bodies: preserve the rejected batch and use a separate post-script verification after the creating script returns, followed by a new database audit. Saved analysis projects were opened read-only; pinned GhidraScript/FlatProgramAPI source confirms the transaction nesting.
- Two passes using analysis-adjusted instruction flow still rejected direct JMP entries as terminals. Stop repeating that approach: a separate read-only raw-prototype-flow pass exports 34 more entries with transaction rollback. The rejected/interior/shared-tail cases remain recorded. Initial optional-module launch failed because its private project directory was missing; that setup failure and corrected launch are retained. All native tools/data remain outside this checkout.
- Whole-module exports now cover 42 selected modules; sixteen original errors remain queryable alongside successful supplemental recovery bodies. Exact export accounting does not establish discovery completeness, ABI recovery or translation parity.
- Envmap branch (session 6): a first unmasked build gave every floor/roof with `$normalmapalphaenvmapmask` a full-strength mirror glare (owner report). Envmaps need their masks and, on bump-mapped surfaces, the bump normals. A VMT pass also rejected vector `$envmapsaturation` values; envmap parameters are now float3 and their errors only drop the envmap. An earlier note blamed a UUID fallback handle for the viewmodel blinking; the bisect showed the bloom ordering bug (8ee25d2) instead.
- Viewmodel flicker (owner report 2026-10-08): single-frame captures cannot show per-frame dropouts; the accepted bloom-regression weapons capture hid a bug where the viewmodel vanished on 1-9 of 61 frames. Use `--capture-burst N` with work/publishing/flicker_check.py for anything that can flicker. Bisected to 8ee25d2 (bloom pass not ordered after the main pass); fixed by putting it in Core3dSystems::PostProcess.
- Rejected research approaches: uninitialized debug options failed before recovery evaluation; initializing options fixed the runner. Retyping the VPhysics return alone still failed; unlocking inferred inputs succeeded. The old catalogue builder rejected nested extension binary paths, so a separate path/hash/address ledger was used. An initial newline-sensitive navigator audit falsely flagged 827 mismatches; its rejected report remains saved, and normalized comparison finds 36. Do not reuse rejected results.
- Debug-overhead explanation: packaged builds were already release; development dependency overrides existed. Profiling identified skinning/uploads and out-of-view preparation instead.
- GPU skinning/current bounds, cached PVS and material reuse: measured local gains with inspected actor/eye/monitor regressions. Do not disable simulation or animation to fake FPS.
- Equal reticle sizes: rejected in favor of original measurements; fix native rasterization instead. A retained yellow color filter was too strict because additive background raised green239â€“250; actual dot positions match.
- Hall culling comparison: rejected because its camera intersected a pillar. Valid plaza/spawn comparisons are accepted; do not reuse the bad viewpoint as proof.
- Frozen900-frame benchmark with6000-tick script: rendered but returned incomplete-script error; helper stopped. Excluded from published comparisons. Do not remove completion validation or call it an engine crash.
- Campaign texture-zero assertion: existing station03 errors caused failure; comparing the identical25 errors verified no new asset regression, not successful decoding.
- Kleiner implicit idle: owned ACT_IDLE resolves idle_subtle. Metrocop idle_baton was already valid; do not repeat the mistaken assumption that all literal idle labels are missing.
- Seeded lab/attention fixtures verify selected behavior, not natural broadcast staging or campaign completion. wip/scripted-scenes is still separate/unverified.

## Reproduce from this checkout

```powershell
.\scripts\build-bevy.ps1
.\launch-bevy-borderless.cmd --map d1_trainstation_02
.\launch-bevy.cmd --movement-script test-inputs/bevy-movement.json --capture artifacts/check-movement.png --report artifacts/check-movement.json
.\launch-bevy.cmd --movement-script test-inputs/bevy-entities-weapons.json --capture artifacts/check-weapons.png --report artifacts/check-weapons.json
cargo test --locked --workspace
$env:HL2_ROOT = 'C:/Program Files (x86)/Steam/steamapps/common/Half-Life 2'
cargo test --locked --workspace -- --ignored
cargo clippy --locked --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings
cargo fmt --all --check
```

Expected: controlled replays finish with captures/reports, no station02 texture errors or pose disagreements. F3 gives developer weapons; E uses doors, wheel/slots select, F1 toggles diagnostics and Escape/tilde open pause/console. Console help lists implemented commands; `sv_cheats 1; impulse 101` is supported. Crosshairs are small five-dot reticles. Developer loadouts/landmark replays are not ordinary campaign proof. Add --world-partition only for the experimental path. Retained uses scripts/build.ps1 and launch-borderless.cmd, without an extra view argument.

Template basis: [STATUS handoff](https://github.com/trevaintdead/ai-game-modding-guides/blob/main/templates/STATUS-handoff.md), adapted for a rewrite.
